- **session-summary**: User-invoked session handoff. `/session-summary pause` writes a structured resume file capturing what the session was doing, what changed, what was decided, and what is unfinished, folding any summary already there into a shorter form above it. `/session-summary resume` reads that file back and reports it as a few bullets, without starting any work it names. - **session-summary**: 会话总结与 skills 迭代——把一次 DSH 会话收尾为「做了什么 → 发现的问题与教训 → 当前 skills 的优化点评估 → 实施」，作为迭代本仓库全部技能的工具（session-summary 自身也在迭代范围内）。当用户要求"总结这次会话""看看 skills 有没有可优化的地方""把这次的经验沉淀进技能"时使用——先取证会话实际动作（git log 提交、对话要点），再对照各技能（SKILL.md / references / 脚本）评估：本次暴露的问题对应哪个技能缺什么能力、哪些教训应固化；会话操作的是另一个项目仓库时，还要把该仓库实证过的教训回流进它自己的 `AGENTS.md`（按那个仓库的规范提交）。产出优化点清单（价值 / 成本 / 建议），总结、教训、清单与回流内容先经独立只读子代理对抗性审查，再交用户确认并按对应技能流程实施。边界：只做代码评审用 code-review；只做历史改动用 git-history-rewrite；实施具体优化时按改动性质转 feature-dev（新增技能/能力）、bug-fix（缺陷）、refactor（结构调整）。 ([lldwb/dsh-commander-mode](https://github.com/lldwb/dsh-commander-mode/tree/HEAD/skills/session-summary) · ★2)

- **session-watchdog**: Load when one agent must watch ALL the other Claude Code / Claude desktop sessions and wake the ones that stopped mid-task, ended on an error or rate limit, or wait forever on background work that already died. Without that wake, nobody notices until the user asks "are you stuck?".
